## 65. Stage 28.5 — UI defect batch: dialog legibility, flyout reliability, global search (2026-07-28, code)

Four defects reported by the user in one session, all fixed in `public/app.js` + `public/styles.css` — no Go, no schema change, no new dependency. Item-by-item mechanics in micro_checklist.md Stage 28.5.

- **Popup text invisible in dark mode.** `.custom-dialog-body` had a literal `color: #334155` against the dark `--panel-bg`, ~1.3:1 — the message in every alert/confirm/prompt was effectively unreadable. Rather than patch that one rule, the fix removed the whole class of bug: Stage 28.2's token block claimed in its own comment to be "the single source of color for the whole app", but 25 rules below it still used literal hexes and never re-themed. Added semantic tokens (`--on-primary`, `--text-placeholder`, and `-color`/`-hover`/`-strong`/`-soft-bg`/`-soft-border` sets for danger/warning/success) to all three token blocks and routed every one of those rules — plus 9 inline `style="color:#..."` strings in `app.js` — through them. Light-theme token values are the exact previous hexes, so light mode is unchanged by design. The comment above the dark block is now a stated rule (no literal colours below it) with its two sanctioned exceptions named: the print-only `.receipt`/`.sticker-label` rules, which are always ink-on-paper, and translucent rgba scrims/glows, which read on either theme. That comment is the thing that keeps this fixed for the next feature, not the 25 individual edits.
- **"In POS nothing is working — if I click anything it is not opening anything."** Not a routing bug: driving all 38 sidebar entries programmatically showed 37 navigating correctly (the 38th being Dashboard, already the current view). The flyout was closing before the pointer got to it, so the click landed on the page behind. Four separate causes, each reproduced with a scripted pointer path before and after the fix — a per-container hide timer that called the *global* close (so module A's timer shut module B's just-opened menu); an 8px gap between row and panel that sits outside the container's box, so crossing or pausing in it started the close; `mouseleave` bound alongside `pointerenter`, where pointer events fire ahead of their compatibility mouse events and the leave handler therefore ran *after* the next row had already cancelled the pending close; and sibling entries with no flyout of their own acting as dead zones with nothing listening. Plus a 140ms dwell (500ms when the pointer is measurably heading at the open panel) before a different module can take the menu, so a diagonal reach across the rows in between no longer steals it. Worst realistic case — a continuous diagonal corner-cut — went from 6/34 to 32/34; the ordinary path is 34/34.
- **Long flyouts running off the bottom of the screen.** Found while verifying the above. Flyout height was capped to the space *below* its trigger, so Stock's 12 screens and Setup's ~25 master doctypes put their tail off-screen when anchored low — nominally scrollable, but not actually reachable, since getting to the scrollbar meant leaving the panel. Panels now take their natural height and slide up to stay on screen, scrolling internally only when taller than the viewport.
- **Global search did nothing.** The box promised "Search menu, category, type or HSN" but only filtered a table you already had open — on the dashboard it was inert. It now suggests every matching destination (sidebar entries, including those only reachable by hovering the right module) and every registered record type, ranking name hits above module hits. Picking one dispatches a click on the real sidebar element where one exists, so routing, permission filtering and active-item highlighting stay in a single place rather than being re-implemented in the search path; the index reads the same `perm-hidden`/`module-hidden` classes the sidebar uses, so hidden entries can't leak through it. Reuses the existing `.typeahead-menu` vocabulary; the pre-existing table filtering still runs alongside. The `category`/`HSN` half of the placeholder is deliberately still unimplemented — searching product records across entities needs a new server-side endpoint, which is a feature, not this bug fix.

**Verified**: `node --check` clean; live Playwright against the running dev server as `manager1` — all 38 entries navigate, hover→click 34/34 on a realistic pointer path, the three original flyout repro scenarios all pass, search suggestions and both Enter- and click-to-navigate confirmed, zero console/page errors. Dark and light screenshot-checked with computed contrast ratios recorded in the checklist (dialog body 1.3:1 → 12.99:1).

**Follow-up the same day (28.5.5)**: the user reported two further symptoms immediately after the above landed, and both traced back to the flyout change itself rather than to anything older. The click handler had been written as a toggle, so clicking a module row already hovered open `closed` it - experienced as "it has to be clicked twice or thrice before it opens". A row now only ever opens on click; closing is moving away, clicking outside the nav, or Escape, and the pin bookkeeping the toggle needed is gone. Separately, while a cursor rests on a row no further `pointerenter` ever fires, so anything that closed the menu meanwhile - navigating away from it, an outside click, Escape, or the sidebar re-rendering and swapping the DOM node under a stationary pointer - left it shut with the cursor still sitting on it; `pointermove` now re-opens when nothing is open, so the smallest movement brings it back. 8/8 on a dedicated repro, with the 34/34 navigation pass and the three flyout scenarios re-run green. Worth carrying forward: sidebar module labels are tenant-configurable and did in fact change under this session mid-run (Buying -> Procurement, Accounting -> Financial Accounting), so UI tests here must key off element IDs or DOM order, never the visible label.

## 64. Stage 29 — OMS operations follow-up (2026-07-27, code)

User explicitly authorized the previously identified OMS follow-ups. Added a unified Order Management workbench over the existing generic document API; no duplicate reporting endpoint or table was introduced. Shopify and Unicommerce intake now normalize into `CreateSalesOrder`, preserving their legacy mapping tables while sharing the SalesOrder engine's SKU mapping, validation, allocation, reservation, hold, and idempotency behavior. Shipment handover now creates a single idempotent Draft SalesInvoice only after all fulfillment tasks ship, and tracking delivery dispatches the existing `Order Delivered` notification; invoice posting/settlement remain explicit finance actions to avoid implicit GL writes. Focused and full serialized tests pass; the first full run exposed a transient existing WMS-slotting fixture flake, whose isolated and subsequent full reruns passed. Stage 23.11 was reviewed again and correctly remains a no-op because no paste-grid product surface exists.

Follow-up: flyout navigation now opens directly on pointer entry over each module row/arrow (including the dynamically-rendered Database Schema Design module list), with focus-in support for keyboard users and the existing click interaction retained for touch users. The sidebar's shared stacking layer is above sticky table headers, so no table can paint over an open flyout.

Follow-up: Purchase Requisitions now take their required number exclusively from the tenant's existing configurable `PR` Prefix Config during the authenticated server-side save (using the requester's location and the current Indian financial year); therefore a requester cannot create a duplicate by typing a number. New requirement wording is automatically retained in a `PurchaseRequisitionDescription` master and offered as a soft type-ahead suggestion later. Department remains the existing Core Department master and is now pulled through the same type-ahead control.

## 63. Stage 28 — Configurability, Theming & Deployment (2026-07-27, code, UNCOMMITTED as of this note)

User request (one batch): every operational config should live in the admin UI, module by module — "nothing hardcoded"; a dark/light/system theme toggle; user-controllable report columns saved as profiles (one Universal/shared, one Personal); and Caddy as the reverse proxy for automatic Let's Encrypt TLS. Approved plan built all four in sequence, verifying + checkpointing after each. Full per-item mechanics in micro_checklist.md Stage 28.

- **28.1 Config framework**: new `engines/settings_registry.go` (a generic settings registry mirroring the report registry — `SettingDefinition{Key,Module,Label,Type,Default,...}`, `RegisterSetting`, typed `GetSettingInt/Bool/String`, a per-tenant `system_settings` KV table with an in-process cache + `SetSetting` validation) + `engines/settings_definitions.go` (7 first-wave settings across Loyalty/Inventory/Security, each mapping a former hardcoded constant to a tenant-editable value whose Default equals the old constant, so behavior is unchanged until an admin edits it). Refactored `loyalty.go` (point expiry, earn rate, tier-recompute toggle), `inventory.go`+`fulfillment.go`+the 4 online-order callers (reservation TTL, threaded through `CreateReservation`'s new `expirySec<=0` choke point), `loyalty_redemption_security.go` (OTP validity, daily redemption cap), and user-creation (default idle-timeout). New HR/Admin-only `GET/PUT /api/v1/admin/settings` (`handlers_settings.go`) + a generic module-by-module **Configuration** admin screen (`public/app.js`, renders any registered setting by type). `system_settings` added to `ProvisionTenantSchema`'s clone list. The framework is the single home all future config must use — adding one is a `RegisterSetting` call + a `GetSetting*` read, no new endpoint/UI.
- **28.2 Theme (light/dark/system)**: `public/styles.css`'s existing `:root` token block gained a full dark palette under `:root[data-theme="dark"]` + a `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` block scoped `:root:not([data-theme="light"])` so System follows the OS while explicit choices win; audited + tokenized every structural literal-color background (`#f8fafc`/`#f1f5f9`/`#f3f4f6`/`#e2e8f0`/toggle track) so dark mode is complete, not half-applied (semantic status colors kept literal). Persisted per-user via a new `users.theme_preference` column (following the `idle_timeout_minutes` precedent), surfaced in `/api/v1/me` GET + accepted on PUT (validated light/dark/system); a no-flash inline `<head>` script applies the cached choice before first paint, reconciled with the server value on load. A 3-segment toggle in the account popover.
- **28.3 Report column profiles**: new `ReportColumnProfile` doctype (mirrors `ReportFilterPreset`) + a "Columns" chooser on the report catalog (show/hide + ↑/↓ reorder, no drag lib) + a Column Profile picker. Two scopes: Personal (owner-filtered client-side) and Universal (shared) — Universal creatable only by HR/Admin or Store Manager, enforced client-side AND server-side at the generic-doc create choke point (`handlers_core_doc_engine.go`).
- **28.4 Caddy/TLS app-side support**: a concurrent session had already built the full `deploy/` config (Caddyfile with automatic Let's Encrypt, systemd unit, env example, migrate/backup scripts, README) — this pass added the missing app-side support and reconciled with it: a new `HOST` env (`routes.go`) to bind the listener loopback-only behind the proxy (default unset = historical all-interfaces bind, no behavior change), and `TRUST_PROXY` + a `clientIP()` helper (`middleware.go`) so the per-IP rate limiter reads the real client from `X-Forwarded-For` behind a trusted proxy instead of collapsing every client into Caddy's loopback bucket. Added `HOST=127.0.0.1`/`TRUST_PROXY=1` to `deploy/erp.env.example`; the rest of `deploy/` left untouched (concurrent-session work).
- **Verified**: `go build`/`go vet`/`node --check`/`go test ./... -p 1` clean (new `TestSettingsRegistry`; two transient shared-DB failures during one full-suite run were confirmed transient — passed in isolation and on rerun, matching the documented finance-GL/WMS shared-DB race). Live-verified each feature over real HTTP on throwaway ports against the dev DB, plus real-browser Playwright passes: the Configuration screen + dark dashboard render correctly (zero console errors), the column chooser hides/reorders + a Universal profile applies exactly, settings GET/PUT/validation/403-gating work, `/me` theme round-trips, the server binds `127.0.0.1` under HOST, and TRUST_PROXY rate-limiting buckets correctly by X-Forwarded-For (5×401 then throttled for one IP, fresh bucket for another). All scratch tooling/test data/DB overrides removed afterward. One deploy step still needs the user: real Let's Encrypt issuance requires their public domain + DNS (`caddy validate` deferred — Caddy isn't installed locally).

For the current build tracker, see **[docs/micro_checklist.md](micro_checklist.md)**.
